Theatrum Chemicum Britannicum (Elias Ashmole (editor), 1652)
==========================================================
Ashmole's 1652 anthology of English alchemical verse, preserving Norton, Ripley, Lully and the canon of English alchemy. Public domain. Digitized from Internet Archive (https://archive.org/details/theatrumchemicum00ashm).
